\displaystyle\cup\left\{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n-1}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\,\middle|\,C,(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{1}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}^{\prime})\right\}

(Simplifying the separating multiplication)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\inf\left\{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n-1}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\,\middle|~\,C,(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{1}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}^{\prime})\right\}

(Emptyset eliminiation)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\inf\left\{1\,\middle|~\,C,(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{1}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}^{\prime})\right\}

(Criteria assumption)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~1~.

We have that for all
h_{\xi}
with
\xi(s,h_{\xi})=1
the above holds, therefore we can now combine it with the magic wand and receive

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s,h)=\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n-1}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)(s,h)=1~.

Lastly, we can put everything together:

\displaystyle\psi~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)

iff

\displaystyle\forall(s,h).~\psi(s,h)=1~\Rightarrow~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s,h)=1

(Both sides are qualitative)

iff

\displaystyle\forall(s,h).~\psi(s,h)=1~\Rightarrow~\inf\{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s,h)\mid n\in\mathbb{N}\}=1

(
\Cref
lem:alternate-wslp)

iff

\displaystyle\forall(s,h).~\psi(s,h)=1~\Rightarrow~\forall n\in\mathbb{N}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\varphi\mid\xi\right)(s,h)=1

(Analysis)

iff

\displaystyle\forall(s,h).~\psi(s,h)=1~\Rightarrow~\forall n\in\mathbb{N}.~\text{safe}_{n}(C,s,h,\xi,\varphi)

(See above)

iff

\displaystyle\xi\models\{\psi\}\;C\;\{\varphi\}

(
\Cref
def:safe-judgement)

Thus finishing the proof.
∎

Appendix E
Soundness Proofs for all Proof Rules

Lemma 44

(Monotonicity of
wrlp
)
.

If
Y\leq Z
then
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove by induction on
n
that
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
. If this holds, the inequality for their limits hold as well.

For the base case
n=0
we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
.

We now assume that the claim holds for some arbitrary but fixed
n
as our induction hypothesis.

For
n+1
we have two cases. The case
C=\;\downarrow\;
is again trivial, since
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=Y\leq Z=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
. Finally, for
C\neq\;\downarrow\;
we have:

\displaystyle~Y\leq Z

implies

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)

(Induction Hypothesis)

implies

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi

(Monotonicity of
\mathbin{{\star}}
)

implies

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Monotonicity of
step
, see
\Cref
lem:monotone-of-step)

implies

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

\displaystyle~\leq\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Monotonicity of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
)

implies

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

This concludes the proof.
∎

Definition 45
.

A program
C
is a terminating atom if for all stack heap pairs
s,h
, all enabled actions
a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))
and probabilities
p
we have

C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\qquad\text{or}\qquad C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~\textnormal{{abort}}~.

Lemma 46
.

For terminating atom
C
, if
X\leq\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
then also
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We show that the statement
X\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
holds for all
n
. For
n=0
we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1
, thus the claim holds immediately.
Since
C
is a terminating atom, we have
\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~Y\right)=\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
.
With this, we can prove the case
n=1
:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(1\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(1\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(
C
is a terminating atom)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(1\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(
\Cref
lem:multiplestep_framing)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(Monotonicity)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~X

(Assumption)

and
n>1
:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n-1}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
,
C
is a terminating atom and
n-1>0
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(
C
is a terminating atom)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(
\Cref
lem:multiplestep_framing)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~X

(Assumption)

Thus, the proof is finished.
∎

Lemma 47
.

If
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
and
Y~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
then also for the sequential composition
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove by induction on
n
that

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)~.

If this holds, the inequality also holds for their limits. Finally, we can use monotonicity of
wrlp
to prove that the claim holds.

For the induction base with
n=0
, we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)=1=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)
.

We assume that the claim holds for some arbitrary but fixed
n
as the induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have two cases, for
C_{1},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C_{1}^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})
either
C_{1}^{\prime}=\;\downarrow\;
or
C_{1}^{\prime}\neq\;\downarrow\;
. We will only consider the case
C_{1}^{\prime}\neq\;\downarrow\;
, since the other is analogous. Then we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Monotonicity of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
and
\dagger
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)

(Antitonicity of
wrlp
w.r.t.
n
and monotonicity w.r.t. postexpectation)

For
\dagger
, we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C_{1}^{\prime}}{\,;}~{C_{2}}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\Big|~{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~{C_{1}^{\prime}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}({C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\Big|~{C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~{C_{1}^{\prime}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}({C_{1}}{\,;}~{C_{2}},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Induction hypothesis and monotonicity)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\Big|~C_{1},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C_{1}^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C_{1},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Operational semantics of sequential composition)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Z\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h)

(Definition of
step
)

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Lemma 48
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
,
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
and
X\leq\left[{b}\right]\cdot X_{1}+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot X_{2}
then also
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove that for all
n
we have
\left[{b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
, where
\left[{b}\right](s,h)=1
if
s\in b
and
\left[{\neg b}\right](s,h)=0
if
s\not\in b
. Then the inequality holds also for their limits and due to monotonicity of all operations, we also get the claim.

For the case
n=0
, we have that
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1\geq\left[{b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
, thus the claim holds.

For the case
n+1
, we have two sub-cases. Either
s\in b
or
s\not\in b
. We will only prove the first, since the latter is analogous. There we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

(Monotonicity w.r.t
n
)

For
\dagger
, we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum\Big[1\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

\displaystyle~\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\text{if-t}]{1}~C_{1},(s,h)\Big]

(
s\in b
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Singleton set)

For
s\not\in b
we similarly have

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{if}}\,\left(\,{b}\,\right)\,\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\,\textnormal{{else}}\,\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\geq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)~.

Combining them, we have the claim from above.
∎

Lemma 49
.

If
I\leq\left[{b}\right]\cdot X+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot Y
and
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)
then for the loop we have
I~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We will now prove by induction on
n
that if
I\leq\left[{b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot Y
then
I\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
. If this holds for all
n
, then also for the limit.

For the base case
n=0
, we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1\geq I
.

Now we assume that for some fixed but arbitrary
n
the claim holds as our induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have two cases. If
s\not\in b
then:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right))(s,h)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi))(s,h)

(
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~Y(s,h)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~(\left[{b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot Y)(s,h)

(Since
s\not\in b
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~I(s,h)

(Assumption)

For
\dagger
, we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(sh)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum\left[1\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\mid\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\text{loop-f}]{1}~\;\downarrow\;,(s,h)\right]

(Operational semantics of loop and
s\not\in b
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Sum over singleton)

Then for
s\in b
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right))(s,h)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C}{\,;}~{\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi))(s,h)

(
\dagger\dagger
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C}{\,;}~{\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

(
\Cref
lem:soundseq)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

(Induction hypothesis and Monotonicity of
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}
w.r.t to postexpectation)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

(
\Cref
lem:alternate-wslp)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~(\left[{b}\right]\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(I\mid\xi\right)+\left[{\neg b}\right]\cdot Y)(s,h)

(Since
s\in b
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~I

(Assumption)

For
\dagger\dagger
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(sh)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum\Big[1\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C}{\,;}~{\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

\displaystyle~\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\text{loop-t}]{1}~{C}{\,;}~{\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}},(s,h)\Big]

(Operational semantics of loop and
s\in b
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket{C}{\,;}~{\textnormal{{while}}\left(\,{b}\,\right)\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Sum over singleton)

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Lemma 50
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
,
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
and
X\leq e_{p}\cdot X_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot X_{2}
then
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove for all
n
that

e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)~.

If this holds, then the same also holds for their limits and thus by monotonicity of all operations the claim is proven.

For the case
n=0
we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1\geq e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

For the case
n+1
we have

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}((e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right))\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(Subdist. of
\mathbin{{\star}}
with plus and monotonicity of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right))

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right))

(Antitonicity of
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}
w.r.t
n
)

Now for
\dagger
:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum\left[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid\left\{\,{C_{1}}\,\right\}\mathrel{\left[\,e_{p}\,\right]}\left\{\,{C_{2}}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[prob]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

(Operational semantics of prob. choice)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~e_{p}(s)\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)+(1-e_{p})(s)\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Operational semantics of prob. choice)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Pointwise operations)

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Lemma 51
.

If
X\mathbin{{\star}}\xi~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\left[{\textsf{{{emp}}}}\right]\right)
then
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove for all
n
that
X\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

For
n=0
we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=1\geq X
.

For
n+1>0
and where
C
is tame we have

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\left[{\textsf{{{emp}}}}\right]\right)

(
\Cref
thm:wlp-wslp-equality)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}(X\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)

(Assumption)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~X

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

For
\dagger
, we have:

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\Bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h))\bigg\}\Bigg)

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]\bigg)

(
\text{Act}(\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h))
is singleton)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\textnormal{{diverge}}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\textnormal{{diverge}},(s,h)\Big]\Bigg)

(Splitting reachable programs)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\Bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\Big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum\Big[p\cdot(1\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\textnormal{{diverge}},(s,h)\Big]\Bigg)

(
\Cref
lem:sounddiv)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\Bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\left[p\cdot(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle+\sum\Big[p\cdot(1\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\Big|~\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\},(s,h)~\xrightarrow[atomic]{p}~\textnormal{{diverge}},(s,h)\Big]\Bigg)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\Bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\left[p\cdot(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle+\sum\Big[p\cdot(1\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\Big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\dots\Big]\Bigg)

(Operational semantics of atomic regions)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\Bigg(\lambda(s,h).~\sum\left[p\cdot(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle+\sum\Big[p\cdot(1)(s,h)\Big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\dots\Big]\Bigg)

(Since there exists
h_{\xi}
with
\xi(s,h_{\xi})=1
and
h=h_{\xi}\mathbin{{\star}}h^{\prime}
for some
h^{\prime}
due to
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wlp
)

The difficulty of this proof would have been far greater, if we had allowed non-tame programs in atomic regions. Since we block when encountering non-tame programs, we have

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)=\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}1=1\geq X
.

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Lemma 52
.

If
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)
then
X\mathbin{{\star}}\xi~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)
.

Proof.

To prove this, we first prove by induction on
n
that for all programs
C
we have the inequality
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)
.

For the induction base
n=0
, the claim holds trivially as
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)=1
.

We now assume that the claim holds for some arbitrary but fixed
n
as our induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have two cases. If
C=\;\downarrow\;
then we have that the equality
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)=Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi
holds. Thus, we assume that
C\neq\;\downarrow\;
. There we prove now

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\left(\pi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)

(
\Cref
eq:combine_magic_wand)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\left(\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)

(Induction Hypothesis)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\mathbin{{\star}}\pi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

and we have

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\leq\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)

iff

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)

by adjointness (cf.
\Cref
eq:adjointness).

Lastly we now have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\pi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi

(See above)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\pi\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~X\mathbin{{\star}}\xi

(Assumption)

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Lemma 53
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)
,
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
and
\text{Write}(C_{i})\cap\text{Vars}\,(C_{3-i},Y_{3-i},\xi)=\emptyset
then
X_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We instead prove that we have

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

by induction on
n
. If this holds for all
n
, the same holds for the limit, which yields the claim.

For the induction base
n=0
, we have

\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)=1=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)~.

We assume that the claim holds for some arbitrary but fixed
n
as our induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have four cases. If
C_{1}=C_{2}=\;\downarrow\;
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket(\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket(\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.)\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Monotonicity of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
and
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}

(
\Cref
eq:reverse_modus_ponens)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

For
\dagger
, we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket(\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.)\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid(\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.),(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}((\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.),(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum\left[\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)\mid(\left.\;\downarrow\;\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.),(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\text{conc-end}]{1}~\;\downarrow\;,(s,h)\right]

(Operational semantics of concurrency)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Sum of singleton)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s,h)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

For the case
C_{1}\neq\;\downarrow\;
and
C_{2}\neq\;\downarrow\;
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\min\bigg\{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}^{\prime}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}^{\prime}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\bigg\}

(
\dagger\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\min\bigg\{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\bigg\}

(Induction Hypothesis and
\text{Vars}\,(C_{i}^{\prime})\subseteq\text{Vars}\,(C_{i})
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\min\bigg\{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\bigg\}

(
\Cref
thm:step_framing and
\text{Vars}\,(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{i}\rrbracket\left(Y_{i}\mid\xi\right))\subseteq\text{Vars}\,(C_{i},Y_{i},\xi)
by
\Cref
lem:overapproximate_wslp)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\bigg\}

(
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
is distributiv with min)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\bigg\}

(
\Cref
eq:sepimp_frame)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\bigg\}

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\bigg\}

(
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}
is antitone w.r.t.
n
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

(Set is a singleton)

For
\dagger\dagger
we prove:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.)\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\big|~(\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.),(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}((\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.),(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\inf\Big\{\sum\big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}^{\prime}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\qquad\big|~C_{1},(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C_{1}^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\Big|~a\in\text{Act}(C_{1},(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}))\Big\},

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\inf\Big\{\sum\big[p\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}^{\prime}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\qquad\big|~C_{2},(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi})~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C_{2}^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\Big|~a\in\text{Act}(C_{2},(s,h\mathbin{{\star}}h_{\xi}))\Big\}\bigg\}

(Operational Semantics of concurrency)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{1}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{1}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}^{\prime}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\quad\qquad\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C_{2}\rrbracket\left(\lambda C_{2}^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket\left.C_{1}\;\middle\|\;C_{2}^{\prime}\right.\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mathbin{{\star}}Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

The cases
C_{1}\neq\;\downarrow\;
,
C_{2}=\;\downarrow\;
and
C_{1}=\;\downarrow\;
,
C_{2}\neq\;\downarrow\;
are analogous to the previous case. We just drop the minimum, since only one thread can be executed.

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

Definition 54
.

A program
C
is almost surely terminating w.r.t.
S
iff for all schedulers
\mathfrak{s}\in S
and all program states
(s,h)
we have
C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{0}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\dots~.

Lemma 55
.

If
X_{1}\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\right)
,
X_{2}\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\right)
and
C
ist almost surely terminating w.r.t.
S
then
a\cdot X_{1}+X_{2}\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(a\cdot Y_{1}+Y_{2}\right)
.

Proof.

We have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(a\cdot Y_{1}+Y_{2}\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(a\cdot Y_{1}+Y_{2})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]+p_{div}

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S~\text{and}~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p_{div}}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\dots\bigg\}

(Definition of
wlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(a\cdot Y_{1}+Y_{2})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

(
C
is almost surely terminating w.r.t.
S
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(a\cdot Y_{1}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}))+Y_{2}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

(Pointwise application)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot a\cdot Y_{1}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\quad~+\sum\left[p\cdot Y_{2}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

(Linearity of sum)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\quad\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot a\cdot Y_{1}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot Y_{2}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

(
\Cref
eq:inf_superlin)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~a\cdot\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot Y_{1}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

\displaystyle~+\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot Y_{2}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[\mathfrak{s}]{p}\hskip-3.01125pt{}^{*}\hskip 3.01125pt~\;\downarrow\;,(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~\mathfrak{s}\in S\bigg\}

(Factorisation with constant)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~a\cdot\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\right)+\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\right)

∎

Lemma 56
.

If
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\left[{\textsf{{{emp}}}}\right]\right)
then
X\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
.

Proof.

Since
S\subseteq\mathbb{S}
we have
\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
. Furthermore, by
\Cref
thm:wlp-wslp-equality we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\left[{\textsf{{{emp}}}}\right]\right)=\textsf{{wlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
. Thus, we also have
X\leq\textsf{{wlp}}^{S}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\right)
.
∎

Lemma 57
.

If
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
and
\text{Write}(C)\cap\text{Vars}\,(Z)=\emptyset
then we also have
X\mathbin{{\star}}Z~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}Z\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

First, it is easy to prove that
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}Z\mid\xi\right)=\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket(\left.C\;\middle\|\;\;\downarrow\;\right.)\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}Z\mid\xi\right)
. From this, we directly have the conclusion by
\Cref
lem:soundconcur.
∎

Lemma 58
.

If
C
is a terminating atom and
X\mathbin{{\star}}\xi~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\mid\left[{\textsf{{{emp}}}}\right]\right)
then
X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

Since
C
is a terminating atom, we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)=\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)
and by
\Cref
lem:soundatomicregion together with the assumption we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\textnormal{{atomic}}\left\{\,{C}\,\right\}\rrbracket\left(Y\mid\xi\right)\geq X
, which proves the claim.
∎

Lemma 59
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)
and
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
then we have for the maximum
\max\left\{\,{X_{1}},\>{X_{2}}\,\right\}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\max\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

By monotonicity (
\Cref
lem:soundmonotone) we have for both
i\in\{1,2\}
that the inequality
\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{i}\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\max\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)
holds, thus for both
i\in\{1,2\}
the inequality
X_{i}\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\max\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)
also holds. Then we also have that the inequality
\max\left\{\,{X_{1}},\>{X_{2}}\,\right\}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\max\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)
holds.
∎

Lemma 60
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)
,
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
,
\xi
is precise and
C
is not probabilistic then
\min\left\{\,{X_{1}},\>{X_{2}}\,\right\}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{X_{2}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove instead by induction on
n
that the equality

\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)}\,\right\}=\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)

holds. If this holds, the claim also holds.

For the induction base
n=0
, we have that
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)=1
, which is always equal to
\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)}\,\right\}
.

Thus, we assume for some arbitrary but fixed
n
the claim holds as our induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have two cases. For
C=\;\downarrow\;
we have

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)}\,\right\}~.

For
C\neq\;\downarrow\;
we have

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(\min\left\{\,{Y_{1}},\>{Y_{2}}\,\right\}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)}\,\right\}\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Induction Hypothesis)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi}\,\right\}\right)

(
\xi
precise and
\Cref
eq:precise_dist_min)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\min\{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\quad\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\}

(
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\Big\{\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right),

\displaystyle~\qquad~\;\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\Big\}

(
\Cref
eq:sepimp_dist_min)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)}\,\right\}

(Definition of
wrlp
)

For
\dagger
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\min\left\{\,{t(C^{\prime})},\>{t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})}\,\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot\min\left\{\,{t(C^{\prime})},\>{t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})}\,\right\}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\min\left\{\,{t(C^{\prime})},\>{t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})}\,\right\}(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\land C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{1}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\bigg\}

(
C
is not probabilistic)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\min\Big\{\sum\left[p\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right],

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\quad\sum\left[p\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\Big\}

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(
C
is not probabilistic)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\bigg\{\inf\Big\{\sum\left[p\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\Big\},

\displaystyle~\qquad\quad\inf\Big\{\sum\left[p\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\Big\}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\min\left\{\,{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)},\>{\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)}\,\right\}

(Definition of
step
)

∎

Lemma 61
.

If
X_{1}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)
,
X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
,
\xi
is precise and
\text{Write}(C)\cap\text{Vars}\,(e)=\emptyset
then
e_{p}\cdot X_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot X_{2}~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
.

Proof.

We prove by induction on
n
that
e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\leq\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
. If this holds, the claim also holds.

For the induction base
n=0
, we have
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)=1=e_{p}+(1-e_{p})=e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{0}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
.

We now assume that the claim holds for some arbitrary but fixed
n
as our induction hypothesis.

For the induction step, we have three cases. For the case
C=\;\downarrow\;
we have the equality
\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)=e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}=e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)
. For the case
C\neq\;\downarrow\;
,
\text{Act}(C,(s,h))=\emptyset
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)(s,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)(s,h)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}1)(s,h)

(
\text{Act}(C,(s,h))=\emptyset
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~1(s,h)

(Definition of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(e_{p}+(1-e_{p})\right)(s,h)

(Algebra)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

(Similar to above)

And lastly we have the case that
C\neq\;\downarrow\;
,
\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\neq\emptyset
:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(e_{p}\cdot Y_{1}+(1-e_{p})\cdot Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\left(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

(Induction Hypothesis)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~e_{p}\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)+(1-e_{p})\cdot(\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi)\right)

(
\xi
is precise and
\Cref
eq:precise_dist_plus,eq:precise_dist_mult)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\big(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)

\displaystyle~\quad~+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\big)

(Monotoncitiy of
\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}
and
\dagger
, see below)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~e_{p}\cdot\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)

\displaystyle+(1-e_{p})\cdot\left(\xi\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)\mathbin{{\star}}\xi\right)\right)

(
\Cref
eq:sepimp_superdist_plus,eq:magicwand_superdist_mult)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{1}\mid\xi\right)+(1-e_{p})\cdot\textsf{{wrlp}}_{n+1}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(Y_{2}\mid\xi\right)

(Definition of
wrlp
)

Lastly for
\dagger
we have:

\displaystyle~\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~e_{p}\cdot t(C^{\prime})+(1-e_{p})\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\left[p\cdot(e_{p}\cdot t(C^{\prime})+(1-e_{p})\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime}))(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\mid C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\right\}

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot(e_{p}(s^{\prime})\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})+(1-e_{p}(s^{\prime}))\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}))

\displaystyle~\qquad\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Pointwise application)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot(e_{p}(s)\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})+(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime}))

\displaystyle~\qquad\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(
\Cref
lem:expectation_stackequal,lem:onestep_stackequal and
\text{Write}(C)\cap\text{Vars}\,(e_{p})=\emptyset
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot e_{p}(s)\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})+p\cdot(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})

\displaystyle~\qquad\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Distributivity)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot e_{p}(s)\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad+\sum\big[p\cdot(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Commutativitiy)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot e_{p}(s)\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

\displaystyle~+\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(
\Cref
eq:inf_superlin and
\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\neq\emptyset
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~e_{p}(s)\cdot\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot t(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

\displaystyle~+(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot\inf\bigg\{\sum\big[p\cdot t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big|~C,(s,h)~\xrightarrow[a]{p}~C^{\prime},(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle~\qquad\bigg|~a\in\text{Act}(C,(s,h))\bigg\}

(Constant factors can be shifted outside)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~e_{p}(s)\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~t(C^{\prime})\right)(s,h)+(1-e_{p}(s))\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})\right)(s,h)

(Definition of
step
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~t(C^{\prime})\right)+(1-e_{p}\cdot\textsf{{step}}\llbracket C\rrbracket\left(\lambda C^{\prime}.~t^{\prime}(C^{\prime})\right)\right)(s,h)

(Pointwise application)

Thus, the claim is proven.
∎

See
4

Proof.

We prove the soundness of all inference rules.

term

X~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\;\downarrow\;\rrbracket\left(X\mid\xi\right)

Y\leq\sup_{v\in\mathbb{Z}}\left[{e\mapsto v}\right]\mathbin{{\star}}(\left[{e\mapsto v}\right]\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}X\left[{x}\coloneqq{v}\right])

look

Y~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket x\mathrel{\textnormal{{:=}}}\texttt{<}\,e\,\texttt{>}\rrbracket\left(X\mid\xi\right)

Y\leq\inf_{v\in\mathbb{Z}}\left[{v\mapsto e_{1},\dots,e_{n}}\right]\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}X\left[{x}\coloneqq{v}\right]

alloc

Y~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket{x}\mathrel{\textnormal{{:=}}}\mathtt{new}\left(e_{1},\dots,e_{n}\right)\rrbracket\left(X\mid\xi\right)

Y\leq X\left[{x}\coloneqq{e}\right]

assign

Y~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket x\mathrel{\textnormal{{:=}}}e\rrbracket\left(X\mid\xi\right)

Y\leq\left[{e\mapsto\,{-}\,}\right]\mathbin{{\star}}(\left[{e\mapsto e^{\prime}}\right]\mathbin{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{$\boldsymbol{{-}\hskip-2.44444pt{-}}$}}\hskip-4.30554pt\text{\raisebox{0.55974pt}{\rotatebox{-17.0}{$\star$}}}}X)

mut

Y~\leq~\textsf{{wrlp}}\llbracket\texttt{<}\,e\,\texttt{>}\mathrel{\textnormal{{:=}}}e^{\prime}\rrbracket\left(X\mid\xi\right)
